Tell: track record

Every message Tell posted, as it was sent, with the price 1 hour, 4 hours and 24 hours later (Hyperliquid 1-minute candles). Nothing is edited or removed; outcomes fill in as time passes. Watchlist updates for Pro members appear 24 hours after they were sent. Lines from third-party sources that have not yet confirmed permission (prediction markets, Coinalyze) are left out and marked. Times in UTC. Information, not advice.
